### [1] [SynWeaver: Website-Prior Task and Trajectory Co-Synthesis for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12429)
*Ruitao Wang,Yuwen Hao,Menglin Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SynWeaver提出一种结合网站先验的任务—轨迹协同合成框架，通过结构化探索、网站地图构建、任务生成与轨迹联合修正，提升Web代理在已知和未知网站上的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有探索式数据合成方法存在两项不足：难以覆盖网站的完整功能，且缺乏网站先验时容易生成幻觉任务，导致轨迹合成的多样性、有效性和效率受限。

Method: 首先对目标网站进行结构化探索，构建覆盖多种页面状态和可执行交互的网站地图；随后从页面级和转移级信息中提取监督信号，训练具备网站特定先验的UI感知模型，以生成更 grounded 的任务；最后协同合成任务与执行轨迹，在二者不一致时联合更新，并对结果进行验证和修复，生成可执行且语义一致的监督数据。

Result: 在WebArena和WebVoyager上的实验表明，SynWeaver持续优于具有竞争力的数据合成基线，并为域内和域外泛化提供了更有效的监督信号。摘要未报告具体性能提升幅度。

Conclusion: 通过将网站结构化探索、网站先验建模以及任务—轨迹协同优化结合起来，SynWeaver能够提高功能覆盖率、降低幻觉任务生成，并增强Web代理的泛化性能。

Abstract: Web agents often struggle to generalize to unseen websites because they lack website-specific supervision. Recent exploration-based data synthesis methods reduce manual annotation, but they still face two key limitations: they often fail to cover the full functionality of a website, and without sufficient website prior knowledge, they tend to propose hallucinated tasks, which in turn limits the diversity and efficiency of downstream trajectory synthesis. We present \textbf{SynWeaver}, a website-prior task-trajectory co-synthesis framework designed to address these challenges. SynWeaver first performs structured website exploration and constructs a website map that covers a broad set of functionally distinct page states and executable interactions on the target website. It then derives page-level and transition-level supervision from this map to train a UI-aware model with website-specific priors, enabling more grounded task proposals. Finally, SynWeaver performs collaborative task-trajectory synthesis, jointly updating the task and execution trajectory when they become inconsistent, and then verifies and repairs the collected results to produce executable, semantically aligned supervision. Experiments on WebArena and WebVoyager demonstrate that SynWeaver consistently outperforms strong synthesis baselines and yields more effective supervision for both in-domain and out-of-domain generalization.

</details>


### [2] [Specification-first convergence with an AI coding agent: a case study of dismantling a core architectural invariant across 189 files in a 717k-line codebase with no test oracle and no human code review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12440)
*Joel Abenhaim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个AI编码代理在规格优先协议下，对717,725行TypeScript生产代码库实施大规模架构重构的单案例研究。代理在无人审查生成代码、且缺乏预先行为预言机的情况下，成功拆除了核心生命周期不变量，使流式生成可在界面面板关闭后继续，并在重新打开时恢复连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在评估AI编码代理处理传统上被认为需要重写、难以通过渐进式重构完成的大规模架构变更的能力，并探索形式化规格、反复审计和自动反馈能否降低复杂重构风险。

Method: 研究采用规格优先协议：由代理生成形式化规格，开展14轮规格与源代码对照审计，随后进行原子化实现、编译与测试反馈循环，再开展17轮代码与冻结规格对照审计。共31轮审计，采用连续两轮零缺陷作为收敛标准，并公开规格和原始会话日志以支持复核。

Result: 重构涉及189个文件，连同提取阶段共涉及288个文件，新增34,770行、删除16,422行。审计过程中共修正201个缺陷；在首次及随后约30次会话中，作者报告软件均按规格运行且未观察到错误。项目耗时三天，成本约2,430美元。

Conclusion: 该单案例表明，结合冻结规格、多轮自审计和编译测试反馈，AI编码代理可能完成超大规模、跨模块的架构重构。但证据主要来自单一项目和作者报告，缺乏独立人类复核、预先行为预言机及对照实验，因此尚不足以证明该方法具有普遍可靠性或可推广性。

Abstract: This paper reports a single, fully instrumented case study of a large-scale architectural refactoring by an AI coding agent under a specification-first protocol, with no human review of the generated code and no pre-existing oracle to validate the target behaviour. The task, dismantling a central invariant across a large interdependent codebase, was assessed by the author as effectively infeasible through incremental refactoring, the kind of change that conventionally calls for a rewrite instead. Under the protocol described here, the agent completed it successfully.
  The system is a 717,725-line production TypeScript application across 3,648 files. The task required dismantling a core lifetime invariant: the guarantee that a UI panel remains open for the duration of an AI request. The target behaviour was that a streaming generation survives the closing of its panel and can be reattached, on reopening, to the same live stream with no loss or duplication.
  The protocol: formal specification by the agent, 14 refinement cycles auditing that specification against the source code, atomic implementation, a compile/test feedback loop, then 17 verification cycles auditing the code against the frozen specification. Across 31 audit passes, 201 defects were corrected before any human executed the program. The convergence criterion was empirical: two consecutive verification passes returning zero findings.
  The change touched 189 files (31 new); with the extraction phase, the two commits total 288 files, 34,770 insertions, 16,422 deletions. Across the first and roughly thirty later sessions, the software behaved as specified, no bug observed. Elapsed: three days; cost: USD 2,430.
  The full specification and raw session logs, 1,500+ pages in French, are published as evidence, allowing inspection of the process and submission to a language model for consistency checking.

</details>


### [3] [Does It Render Everywhere? A Study of Cross-Environment Compatibility in MLLM-Generated Webpages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12518)
*Ziyun Guo,Jingyu Xiao,Yuqiang Sun,Yintong Huo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文系统研究了多模态大语言模型生成网页在不同浏览器与设备环境下的渲染兼容性，并提出数据集WebCompat和离线检测器XCompat。结果表明，68%的生成网页存在至少一种跨环境兼容性问题；XCompat在测试集上取得0.903的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页生成评估通常只在固定浏览器和设备配置下衡量视觉保真度，无法反映真实部署中的跨环境渲染可靠性。论文旨在填补这一评估缺口，量化AI生成前端页面的兼容性风险，并定位其可感知表现与代码层成因。

Method: 作者构建WebCompat数据集，包含2,032个标注实例：由8种代表性AI工具生成的网页，在9种浏览器—设备组合中进行渲染。研究据此分析兼容性问题的发生比例、用户可见症状和代码级根因；进一步设计XCompat检测器，联合页面截图的视觉信息与DOM树结构信息，以离线方式识别兼容性问题，并与现有检测工具及大语言模型基线比较。

Result: 68%的AI生成网页至少出现一种兼容性问题。最常见的是破坏整体布局的问题，占问题症状的88.3%，主要表现为页面直接缩放以适配屏幕而导致字体过小，或缩放比例不匹配造成内容被截断。图像变形、组件缺失等局部元素问题相对较少，占13.4%。尽管多数模型会生成响应式设计相关模式，但其实现往往不正确。XCompat在WebCompat-test上达到0.903的F1分数，优于已有兼容性检测工具和大语言模型基线。

Conclusion: AI生成网页的跨环境兼容性是普遍且重要的可靠性问题，单一环境下的视觉保真评估不足以衡量实际可用性。WebCompat为该问题提供了规模化评测基准，XCompat则证明融合视觉截图和DOM结构能够有效检测问题。该工作建议后续前端代码生成系统将响应式实现正确性与多环境渲染测试纳入生成、评估和修复流程。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have been increasingly adopted to automate webpage generation from visual designs (e.g., screenshots). However, existing evaluations are limited to visual fidelity assessment under a fixed browser-device configuration. Such a setting overlooks the cross-environment rendering compatibility for real-world deployments.
  To address this gap, we present the first systematic empirical study of cross-environment compatibility in AI-generated webpages. Specifically, we construct WebCompat, a dataset of 2,032 annotated instances, comprising webpages generated by 8 representative AI tools, each rendered across 9 browser-and-device combinations. We analyze the prevalence of compatibility issues, their user-perceptible symptoms, and underlying code-level root causes. Our findings reveal that 68% of generated webpages exhibit at least one compatibility issue, underscoring the pervasive reliability concerns surrounding MLLM-generated front-end artifacts. The most prevalent symptoms are failures that disrupt the entire page layout (88.3%): pages shrink directly to fit the target screen with too small fonts, or exhibit scale mismatches that produce cut-off content. Failures localized to individual elements, such as image distortion or missing components, are comparatively less common (13.4%). Furthermore, although most MLLMs incorporate responsive design patterns into the generation, they fail to properly implement these codes.
  Guided by the findings, we develop XCompat, a lightweight offline compatibility issue detector that combines visual screenshots and the structural DOM tree for analysis. It achieves an F1 score of 0.903 on the WebCompat-test, outperforming the existing compatibility checking tools and LLM baselines. All datasets and tools are released to support future research on rendering reliability in MLLM-based front-end code generation.

</details>


### [4] [CAPRI: Contract-Aware Proof Repair for Isabelle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13459)
*Jim Woodcock,Gabriel Leite,Augusto Sampaio,Ran Wei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出CAPRI，一种面向Isabelle证明修复的契约感知工作流：除由Isabelle验证证明可接受性外，还由独立检查器验证LLM是否仅修改获授权区域。实验显示，受限于“仅证明体”的接口可在零契约违规下取得较高修复率，但完整理论可编辑的迭代流程存在越权修改风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Isabelle构建成功只能说明提交的理论被接受，不能证明LLM未改动开发者禁止修改的文本。对于自动化证明修复，尤其在既有形式化开发中，需同时保障证明正确性、编辑范围合规性以及过程可审计性。

Method: CAPRI将机器可读的编辑契约与证明修复流程结合：LLM生成修改建议后，Isabelle检查理论是否通过，独立检查器核验候选仓库是否满足编辑契约；同时保留提示词、提案、候选仓库、诊断信息、裁决结果和哈希以支持审计。评估覆盖4个开发项目中的12个失败证明、5种工作流、每个任务与条件3次重复，共180次运行；并比较单次修复、迭代修复、仅证明体编辑、完整理论编辑及不同模型/配置。

Result: 180次运行中获得138个有效修复。144个最终候选被Isabelle接受，其中6个修改了受保护文本，且均出现在允许编辑完整理论的迭代工作流中。仅证明体接口达到29/36个有效修复且无契约违规；对应的完整理论工作流为31/36。单次修复为22/36，后续预先冻结的迭代工作流为32/36，但这些数字比较的是完整工作流而非单一机制。事后OpenRouter实验未改善指定的Luna比较；使用匹配示例的Sol配置为33/36，高于冻结OpenAI Responses条件的29/36，但单侧精确McNemar检验未达统计显著性（p=0.0625）。

Conclusion: CAPRI表明，证明通过与编辑合规应被视为两个独立验证目标。限制LLM仅编辑证明体会略微降低修复数量，却能消除实验中观察到的契约违规，因此为高可信形式化证明修复提供了有价值的安全—效果折中。模型或配置间的表面差异尚缺乏统计显著证据，后续应扩大样本并进一步分离工作流设计、模型能力和示例配置的影响。

Abstract: We address the use of large language models (LLMs) to help discover Isabelle proofs. An Isabelle build establishes that the submitted theory is accepted, but not that an LLM changed only what the developer authorised. We present CAPRI, a contract-aware repair workflow in which Isabelle checks the proof and an independent checker enforces a machine-readable edit contract. Prompts, proposals, candidate repositories, diagnostics, verdicts, and hashes are retained for audit. We evaluate five workflows on twelve failed proofs from four developments, with three replicates per task and condition, giving 180 runs and 138 valid repairs. Of 144 terminal candidates accepted by Isabelle, six had modified protected text; all arose in iterative workflows that could edit a complete theory. A proof-body-only interface produced 29/36 valid repairs and no contract violations, compared with 31/36 for the corresponding full-theory workflow. One-shot repair produced 22/36, while a later prospectively frozen iterative workflow produced 32/36; these figures compare complete workflows rather than individual mechanisms. A separate post hoc OpenRouter campaign found no improvement in the designated Luna comparisons. A Sol configuration with matched demonstrations produced 33/36 repairs, compared with 29/36 in the frozen OpenAI Responses condition, but the difference was not statistically significant in a one-sided exact McNemar test ($p=0.0625$).

</details>


### [5] [Memorization Diagnostics for Code LLMs Should be Scale-Aware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12771)
*Prateek Kumar Rajput,Abdoul Aziz Bonkoungou,Alberick Euraste Djiré,Xunzhu Tang,Yewei Song,Iyiola Emmanuel Olatunji,El Hacen Diallo,Jacques Klein,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文指出，现有用于检测代码大语言模型记忆的探针在模型规模扩大后显著失效，因为它们混淆了表征负荷与记忆。通过对数值问题施加可逆变换，研究发现大型编码器能够适应不同表面形式并保持正确解法族，说明功能泛化能力可能比判断答案是否被直接记忆更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍声称代码大语言模型存在广泛记忆，但传统扰动探针和基于对数概率的探针在大规模模型上难以有效识别记忆，甚至在已知污染的数据集上也表现不佳。因此，有必要解释探针对小模型有效而对大模型失效的原因，并区分表征负荷与真正的记忆。

Method: 研究采用可逆数学变换处理数值问题，以改变输入的表面形式而不改变其潜在解法结构，从而分别考察模型对表征变化的适应能力和对训练样本的记忆依赖。该方法用于评估传统编码器式扰动探针与解码器式对数概率探针所遗漏的现象。

Result: 结果表明，传统同义词扰动、死代码插入等编码器式探针，以及依赖对数概率的解码器式探针，在大规模模型上检测能力明显下降。规模化编码器能够承载较高的表征负荷，并在输入形式变化后仍收敛到正确的解法族。数据污染可能抬高基准成绩，但不能单独证明模型缺乏功能泛化能力。

Conclusion: 评估代码大语言模型时，应将表征负荷与记忆分离，而不能把二者视为同一现象。对于软件工程应用，模型能否适应不同表面形式并生成可用、正确的功能性答案，可能比答案是否曾在训练中出现更具实际意义。未来基准测试应设计能够明确区分记忆与表征泛化的评估方法。

Abstract: The extent to which large language models for code rely on memorization over genuine understanding remains highly debated. While current literature frequently reports widespread memorization, evaluating the underlying probing techniques across dense architectures reveals a severe breakdown in their utility at scale. Traditional encoder-style probes using perturbations such as synonym fuzzing or dead-code insertion struggle to expose memorization in scaled models, even on known-contaminated benchmarks, and decoder-style probes that rely on log probabilities show similar performance degradation. The specific mode of failure for these probes, particularly why such techniques disrupt smaller models but fail to impact larger ones, motivates us to untangle representation load from memorization rather than treating them as a single phenomenon. By applying invertible mathematical transforms to numeric problems, we isolate these two factors and reveal that scaled encoders successfully absorb substantial representation load while still converging on the correct family of solutions. In practical software engineering, this ability to adapt to varying surface forms is what truly matters for usability and generalizability in LLM and agentic applications. Whether a specific solution was seen during training becomes a much less pressing question because although memorization inflates scores on contaminated benchmarks, factoring out representation load makes it debatable how much we should truly care if a functional answer was originally memorized. Future evaluations must therefore be built around separating these phenomena rather than relying on methodologies that quietly entangle them.

</details>


### [6] [Dissecting Software Graphs: Structural Insights for Driver-Guided Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12859)
*Baihong Chen,Hua Ming,Weifeng Pan,Tian Xie,Haipeng Cai,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究多驱动器模糊测试如何覆盖具有多种执行模式的软件结构。基于静态调用图与驱动特定的动态覆盖，作者构建驱动诱导子图，并在27个开源项目上发现多驱动器能显著扩大结构覆盖和缺陷发现范围，但不同驱动器贡献高度不均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模糊测试评估主要关注代码覆盖率和缺陷数量，难以解释不同命令行选项、子命令或配置模式如何划分、重叠及遗漏软件结构，也无法明确这些差异对测试效果的影响。因此，本文旨在从结构视角系统分析多驱动器模糊测试。

Method: 作者以静态调用图作为共享结构骨架，将各驱动器的动态覆盖映射到该骨架上，生成驱动诱导子图。整体方法包含骨架构建、模糊测试与运行剖析、基于图的结构分析，以及面向研究问题的评估四个阶段。实验覆盖27个OSS-Fuzz衍生的C/C++项目、43个可执行文件和854种驱动器配置，并在相同总测试预算下比较多驱动器与最佳单驱动器基线。

Result: 多驱动器模糊测试使调用图节点覆盖率提高27.9%，控制流图边覆盖率提高73.5%，并发现11个单驱动器大多遗漏的独特缺陷或异常行为。驱动器之间的贡献明显不均衡，其诱导子图在凝聚性、碎片化、模块化和重叠程度方面存在显著差异；剩余未探索区域呈现出若干反复出现的结构类型，而非均匀分布的覆盖尾部。

Conclusion: 多驱动器模糊测试不仅是增加测试入口数量的问题，更是一个结构探索问题。静态调用图与动态覆盖相结合的分析框架能够揭示驱动器间的互补性、冗余性及结构盲区，并为驱动器选择、资源分配和后续覆盖优化提供依据。

Abstract: Many software systems expose multiple execution modes through command-line options, subcommands, and configuration flags. For such programs, fuzzing depends on both mutated inputs and the invoked mode. Yet evaluations still focus on coverage and bug counts, leaving unclear how execution modes partition, overlap, and miss software structure, and how these differences affect effectiveness. We present an empirical study of software structure under multi-driver fuzzing. We propose a structural abstraction that uses a static call graph as a shared backbone and projects driver-specific dynamic coverage onto it to derive driver-induced subgraphs. Based on this abstraction, we develop a four-phase methodology for backbone construction, fuzzing and profiling, graph-based analysis, and research-question-driven evaluation. We apply it to 27 OSS-Fuzz-derived C/C++ projects, spanning 43 executables and 854 driver configurations. Under the same total budget, multi-driver fuzzing outperforms the best single-driver baseline, increasing covered call-graph nodes by 27.9% and CFG-edge coverage by 73.5%, and revealing 11 unique bugs and abnormal behaviors largely missed by single-driver fuzzing. However, driver contributions are uneven, subgraphs differ substantially in cohesion, fragmentation, modularity, overlap, and residual under-exploration follows recurring regimes rather than a homogeneous tail. These results show that multi-driver fuzzing is fundamentally a structural exploration problem.

</details>


### [7] [Requirements-Augmented Generation for Trustworthy Acceptance Testing of LLM-Based Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12970)
*Fanyu Wang,Chetan Arora,Zhenping Xie,Yonghui Liu,Kla Tantithamthavorn,Aldeida Aleti,Siwei Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出面向大语言模型软件（LBS）的自动化验收测试框架，通过需求增强生成（REAG）构建上下文感知的测试预言机，并结合置信度校准的级联判定提升判定可靠性。工业案例表明，该方法在预言机质量、准确率和成本效率方面均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LBS具有随机性、上下文依赖性和个性化特征，同一输入在不同用户画像与软件环境下可能需要不同输出，导致传统验收测试和固定测试预言机难以适用。因此，需要能够理解用户意图、适应动态环境并提供可靠判定的自动化测试方法。

Method: 框架包含两个核心部分：一是REAG，通过自适应检索增强生成，从软件需求、领域知识和用户画像中检索相关信息，并结合自推理生成上下文感知的测试预言机；二是置信度校准的级联判定，通过模拟专家一致性量化判定可靠性，对高置信度结果直接接受，对模糊结果升级处理，对不确定结果 abstain，并利用保序风险控制提供经验可靠性保证。

Result: 在生产级营养咨询应用的工业案例中，REAG获得3.91/5的预言机质量评分，82%的案例达到合格或边缘合格水平。置信度校准级联判定达到98.8%的准确率，将预言机质量从3.91提升至4.30，并相较单一判定器基线提高31.7%的成本效率。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将需求与上下文检索、意图推理及置信度校准相结合，能够有效缓解LBS验收测试中的预言机不足问题。该框架在可靠性、输出质量和测试成本之间实现了较好平衡，具备工业应用潜力，但其泛化能力仍需在更多领域、任务和软件环境中进一步验证。

Abstract: LLM-based software (LBS) integrates large language models as core components to deliver flexible, personalised responses. Unlike traditional software with deterministic outputs, LBSs exhibit context-dependent, stochastic behaviour that renders classical acceptance testing and test oracles insufficient: the same query may require fundamentally different responses depending on user personas and software context. This gap creates an urgent need for automated acceptance testing frameworks that autonomously interpret user instructions, while reliably inferring user intentions in a changing environment. In this paper, we present an automated acceptance testing framework for LBS with calibrated verdict reliability via two technical contributions. First, we introduce Requirements-Augmented Generation (REAG), which interprets user intentions by retrieving relevant software requirements, domain knowledge, and personas via adaptive RAG and self-reasoning to generate context-aware test oracles. Second, recognising that oracle generation may retrieve irrelevant constraints, misinterpret intent, or hallucinate requirements, we introduce a confidence-calibrated cascade judgment. This method quantifies verdict reliability via simulated expert agreement -- accepting high-confidence verdicts, escalating ambiguous cases, or abstaining when uncertain -- with empirical reliability guarantees backed by conformal risk control. An industrial case study on a production nutrition advisory application demonstrates that REAG achieves a 3.91/5 oracle quality score, reaching qualified or marginal oracle quality in 82% of cases. The confidence-calibrated cascade achieves 98.8% accuracy, improves oracle quality from 3.91 to 4.30 by filtering unqualified outputs, and delivers a 31.7% cost-efficiency improvement over single-judge baselines, validating industrial viability

</details>


### [8] [How Powerful are LLMs in Generating Formal Program Specifications?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13077)
*Fanpeng Yang,Xing Li,Shuling Wang,Jie An,Zeyu Sun,Shenghua Feng,Wenhan Wang,Weiyi Wang,Naijun Zhan,Fanjiang Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出基于Rocq的Coins评测框架，通过在可信测试用例上实例化候选规格并生成具体证明义务，评估大语言模型生成程序规格的质量，避免将证明难度与规格质量混淆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化验证能够提供强正确性保证，但编写精确规格成本高。现有规格评测依赖实现一致性验证或规格语义等价性证明，难度高且难以区分规格本身的质量与验证过程的复杂度，因此需要更直接、可靠且具有区分度的评测方法。

Method: Coins基于Rocq构建评测流程，使用人工编写的Rocq规格和HumanEval任务作为基准，将待评估规格应用于可信测试用例，生成具体证明义务，并依据成功完成的证明提供可靠的质量证据。该方法利用形式推理的不对称性：证明成功具有明确意义，而证明失败通常存在歧义。

Result: 在HumanEval上的大规模实验表明，LLM生成高质量程序规格仍然十分困难；验证复杂度可能掩盖不同模型或规格之间真实的质量差异。基于测试用例的形式推理比单纯依赖验证难度更能区分规格质量和模型能力。

Conclusion: 规格生成能力的准确评估比单纯扩大模型规模更关键。Coins说明，结合可信测试用例与具体证明义务的评测方式，能够更忠实地衡量LLM在程序规格综合方面的进展。

Abstract: Formal verification provides strong guarantees of software correctness, but its adoption is limited by the high cost of writing precise formal specifications. While recent large language models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in theorem proving and verified code generation, their true ability to generate program specifications remains unclear. Existing evaluations require either verifying implementation conformance or proving semantic equivalence between specifications, both of which are formidably difficult and may conflate proof difficulty with specification quality. To address this problem, we introduce Coins, a Rocq based evaluation framework that assesses specification quality by instantiating specifications under evaluation on trusted test cases and generating concrete proof obligations. This design aligns with the asymmetric nature of formal reasoning, where successful proofs provide reliable evidence while proof failures are inherently ambiguous. Using Coins, we conduct a large scale study on HumanEval with a curated set of human written Rocq specifications. Our results show that specification generation remains a formidable challenge, and that verification complexity can obscure genuine differences in specification quality. Overall, we find that accurate specification evaluation, rather than model scaling alone, is central to understanding the power of LLMs for specification synthesis, and that test case based formal reasoning offers a more faithful and discriminative measure of progress.

</details>


### [9] [Can Formal Specifications Be Synthesized from Tests Alone?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13240)
*Tianhai Liu,Maximilian Müller,Tobias Hey,Vitus Lüntzel,Muhammad Minhas,Anne Koziolek,Bernhard Beckert*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种黑盒规约推断方法：仅利用测试代码与动态执行轨迹，通过大语言模型生成候选 Java Modeling Language（JML）规约，并以有界模型检查验证和迭代修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手工编写形式化规约成本高。已有基于大语言模型的规约生成通常需要读取实现源码，因知识产权保护、访问权限和部署成本等问题难以在工业场景应用。论文旨在降低对实现内部信息的依赖。

Method: 方法将实现视为黑盒：大语言模型只接收程序接口、选定输入及其输出或状态变化，不访问实现细节。模型据此推断候选规约；随后在本地使用有界模型检查器验证候选规约，并将验证诊断反馈给模型，以支持多轮迭代优化。

Result: 在 SpecGenBench 基准上的初步实验表明，测试与执行轨迹能够引导大语言模型生成具有实际含义的 JML 规约。实验同时发现，模型检查器对规约语法和语义的兼容性，以及高质量、可操作的诊断反馈，是提升迭代可靠性的关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: 测试驱动的黑盒规约推断为在不暴露源码条件下自动生成形式化规约提供了可行路径，具备工业应用潜力；但要实现稳定可靠的自动化效果，仍需改进检查器兼容性、反馈机制与迭代修正能力。

Abstract: Formal specifications offer strong guarantees, but remain costly to write manually. Recent LLM-based approaches automate this by inferring specifications from source code, yet their reliance on white-box access poses barriers to industrial adoption due to intellectual property risks and deployment costs. Our approach uses LLMs to infer candidate specifications solely from test code and dynamic execution traces: the LLM observes only the program interface, selected inputs, and corresponding outputs or state changes, while the implementation internals remain hidden. Candidate specifications are validated locally using bounded model checking, with feedback guiding iterative refinement. Initial results on the SpecGenBench benchmark suggest that tests can guide LLMs towards meaningful Java Modeling Language specifications, while also highlighting checker compatibility and diagnostic feedback as key challenges for reliable refinement.

</details>


### [10] [Refine After Generation: Toward Correct and Concise Patches in LLM-based Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13292)
*Wenqiang Luo,Jacky Keung,Xiaoyu Shi,Yicheng Sun,Boyang Yang,Zhou Yang,Haoye Tian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文指出，现有大语言模型自动程序修复系统虽能解决大量仓库级问题，但其补丁普遍比开发者补丁更冗长、复杂。作者提出生成后补丁精炼框架RECAP，在基本保持甚至提升修复成功数的同时，显著缩减补丁规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有APR评估主要关注测试是否通过或问题是否解决，较少考察补丁的可审查性与最小性。对SWE-bench Verified上28种先进方法的分析表明，即使成功补丁也存在系统性膨胀：中位方法的总改动量、净改动量和圈复杂度均明显高于开发者补丁。作者因此将“补丁冗长性”界定为影响实际维护价值的关键问题。

Method: 作者首先对28个APR方法生成的补丁进行规模与复杂度对比，并分析冗长性的来源。结果认为，迭代式精炼、宽上下文等提升修复能力的设计会诱发冗余，而格式约束或最小化提示等表层控制难以有效解决。随后，论文提出后生成补丁精炼任务，并设计可插拔适配器RECAP：其精炼器利用由多源补丁对构建的数据集，通过监督微调和直接偏好优化训练，并使用蒸馏后的推理轨迹辅助学习。最后，作者在4个宿主修复系统上，与提示工程、提交拆分及最小性感知基线进行比较。

Result: 统计结果显示，中位APR方法相较开发者补丁多出121.78%的总改动、80.91%的净改动，并具有43.99%更高的圈复杂度。相比之下，各类基线虽然能缩小补丁，却会减少49至217个已解决实例。RECAP则展现出更优的规模—正确性权衡：相对开发者补丁，平均总改动偏差由+242.14%降至+4.24%，净改动偏差由+348.24%降至-39.75%；同时在不同宿主系统上可保持或额外提升最多42个问题的解决数量。

Conclusion: 论文的核心结论是，补丁最小化并非简单的语法压缩问题，而与修复生成阶段的能力导向设计存在结构性冲突。将“生成正确补丁”和“生成后精炼补丁”解耦，是在不显著牺牲修复能力的前提下提升补丁简洁性、可读性和审查效率的可行路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have advanced automatic program repair (APR) to the point where agentic systems routinely resolve real-world, repository-level issues. Yet the generated patch has received little scrutiny beyond whether it passes tests.
  In this paper, we identify patch verbosity as a major yet overlooked concern in LLM-based APR. Characterizing 28 state-of-the-art approaches on SWE-bench Verified, we find that even successful patches are consistently larger and more complex than developer patches, with the median approach producing 121.78% more total changes, 80.91% more net changes, and 43.99% higher cyclomatic complexity. We further show that this verbosity is rooted in capability-oriented design choices such as iterative refinement and broad context, and can hardly be reduced by surface-level controls such as output format or minimality prompts. Motivated by these findings, we formulate post-generation patch refinement and propose RECAP, a lightweight, plug-and-play adapter that attaches to existing repair frameworks after generation. RECAP's refiner is trained via supervised fine-tuning and direct preference optimization with distilled reasoning traces, on a dataset of patch pairs we construct from multiple sources. Across four host systems, prompting, commit-untangling, and minimality-aware baselines reduce patch size only by sacrificing 49 to 217 resolved instances. In contrast, RECAP achieves a substantially better size-correctness tradeoff, cutting average total changes from +242.14% to +4.24% and net changes from +348.24% to -39.75% relative to developer patches while preserving or improving resolution by up to 42 instances. Our results indicate that minimality cannot be simply reduced to syntactic compression, and that decoupling minimization from generation offers a practical path to more reviewable repairs.

</details>


### [11] [Integration-First Structural Coverage for Embedded Software:Trace-Based Evidence, Hybrid Runtime Analysis, and Cross-Variant Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13322)
*Alexander Weiss,Albert Schulz,Michael Wittner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向嵌入式系统的集成优先覆盖策略，以设备上的集成测试和系统测试为主要完整性证据，并结合混合运行时分析与Hyper Coverage，实现优化发布构建中的可靠分支、条件和MC/DC覆盖测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统结构覆盖主要在单元级采集，原因是插桩和可观测性成本较低，但这与真实设备上的集成和系统行为不一致。经典插桩会扰动时序、内存占用和并发行为，而仅依赖执行轨迹重建覆盖率时，在编译器激进优化后难以可靠区分决策与条件。

Method: 流程上采用集成优先的覆盖策略，将集成测试和系统测试作为基线，并通过显式闭环处理剩余覆盖缺口，将其标记为已覆盖或有依据地说明。技术上使用嵌入式执行轨迹作为观测路径，引入混合运行时分析（hRA），通过最小且语义保持的可观测性支架，在-O3优化构建的轨迹中保留决策和条件边界，同时将覆盖状态与计数逻辑置于目标设备之外。进一步利用Hyper Coverage合并不同测试层级、测试运行、产品变体和构建配置的覆盖证据。

Result: 该方法将对象代码到源代码的映射从启发式重建转化为可审查证据，使发布近似二进制上的分支覆盖、条件覆盖和MC/DC测量变得可行，并能够识别所有相关变体中始终未被测试的源代码行。

Conclusion: 论文通过流程改进与低扰动追踪分析的结合，缩小了单元级覆盖与系统级真实完整性之间的差距。其核心贡献是以集成和系统测试为主证据、以可解释的缺口闭环和跨配置证据整合支撑覆盖完整性评估。

Abstract: Structural coverage is widely used as evidence that testing is complete, yet in embedded projects it is predominantly collected at unit level, simply because that is where instrumentation and observability are inexpensive. This produces a mismatch. The most representative completeness signal would come from integration and system tests executed on the device under test, but classical instrumentation perturbs timing, memory footprint and concurrency behaviour, while purely trace-reconstructed coverage loses reliability for decisions and conditions as soon as the compiler optimizes aggressively. We address this mismatch from both ends. On the process side we describe an integrationfirst coverage strategy that treats integration and system tests as the baseline measurement and drives the residual gaps through an explicit closure loop, so that completeness is established as covered or justified rather than as covered alone. On the technical side we use embedded trace as the observation path and add hybrid runtime analysis (hRA): a minimal, semantics-preserving observability scaffolding that keeps decision and condition boundaries distinguishable in the trace stream of an optimized (-O3) build, while all coverage state and counting remain off-target. This converts object-to-source mapping from a heuristic reconstruction into reviewable evidence and makes branch, condition and MC/DC measurement practical on release-like binaries. Finally we describe Hyper Coverage, a consolidation layer that merges evidence across test levels, test runs, variants and build configurations, and that exposes source lines which remain untested in every relevant variant.

</details>


### [12] [Does Fixing Break Security? An Empirical Study of Security Degradation in Iterative LLM-Driven Infrastructure-as-Code Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13404)
*Benjamin Agyekum,Fabio Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文首次从逐轮轨迹而非累计最优指标考察大语言模型迭代修复基础设施即代码时的安全回退。严格口径下，约3.3%的场景出现真实安全回退；大多数表面回退源于多资源重构造成的测量现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迭代式修复通常依赖验证器反馈进行多轮改错，但累计最优指标天然单调，掩盖了单次修复可能在解决原问题时破坏先前已通过安全检查的风险。研究旨在量化该风险、识别成因，并给出停止迭代的实践依据。

Method: 基于IaC-Eval的5,968条场景时间线，分析15种配置下最多5轮修复过程，并对具有相邻轮次Checkov数据的4,440个转换进行比较。研究跟踪30个CIS检查标识，按代码差异归因；同时采用标准检测口径与仅计入排他性检查失败的严格检测口径，以区分真实回退与多资源测量伪象。

Result: 标准口径下，13.8%的场景和24.8%的轮次转换至少发生一次安全回退；严格口径下分别降至3.3%和5.2%，说明多数表面回退与多资源重构有关。资源重构占归因的79.0%。发生回退的转换具有2.6倍更高代码变更量（Cohen's d=0.90）和4.9倍更高的严格口径检查波动性（d=1.49）。标准口径回退中36.6%会在平均1.2轮后自行恢复，第3轮是较优停止点。

Conclusion: 迭代式大语言模型IaC修复确会导致安全回退，但以严格、可辩护的定义估计，其发生率约为场景的3.3%。系统应在反馈回路中纳入安全回退检测与约束，并将迭代预算控制在约3轮，以平衡修复收益、代码扰动和安全波动。

Abstract: Background: Iterative feedback loops are the dominant paradigm for improving LLM-generated Infrastructure-as-Code (IaC): validators such as Checkov and terraform validate feed error signals back for successive repair attempts. Prior work reports cumulative-best metrics, which are non-decreasing by construction, so the raw per-iteration security trajectory has never been examined for IaC. Aims: We study security regression (a previously-passing CIS Benchmark check that fails after a repair iteration) to determine whether and how often iterative LLM repair degrades security while fixing other issues. Method: We analyze 5,968 scenario timelines from the IaC-Eval benchmark, each one scenario run through one configuration for up to 5 repair iterations. The 15 configurations (six model-specific RAG, nine model-aggregated non-RAG, three temperatures each) yield 4,440 iteration transitions with Checkov data on both sides. We track 30 individual CIS check IDs and classify root causes from code diffs, under two detection modes: standard (inclusive) and strict (exclusive check failures only). Results: Under standard detection, 13.8% of scenarios (24.8% of transitions) exhibit at least one regression. Under strict detection the rate falls to 3.3% of scenarios (5.2% of transitions), indicating most apparent regressions are multi-resource measurement artifacts. Resource restructuring (79.0%) is the dominant root cause. Regression transitions show 2.6x more code churn (Cohen's d=0.90) and 4.9x higher strict-mode check volatility (d=1.49). Of standard-mode regressions, 36.6% self-correct within an average of 1.2 iterations; iteration 3 is the optimal stopping point. Conclusions: Iterative IaC repair does introduce security regressions, but the conservative, defensible rate is about 3.3% of scenarios. Our findings motivate security-aware feedback-loop design and actionable iteration-budget guidance.

</details>


### [13] [LLM-Assisted Dynamic Threat Analysis for Attacker-Reachable Software Weaknesses in Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13450)
*Md Wasiul Haque,Sagar Dasgupta,Mizanur Rahman,Md Rayhanur Rahman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文评估大语言模型在Autoware自动驾驶软件栈中自动生成动态验证测试工件的能力。结论是：静态候选点生成并非主要瓶颈，真正限制动态验证的是复杂真实工程中的构建集成与依赖配置；在给定预算内，未动态确认任何候选弱点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶软件包含大量影响控制决策的安全关键代码。静态分析虽能发现潜在问题位置，但要确认其可达性与实际影响，仍需可编译、可执行且可模糊测试的验证工件。人工构造此类工件成本高，论文因此考察本地开源权重大语言模型能否自动完成该流程。

Method: 作者对185个软件包进行与编译器语义一致的静态分析，识别1,375条决策规则、2,274项验证检查及482条从输入到安全输出的数据流，并据此建立弱点分类、抽样740个可达候选点。研究比较两种本地开源权重模型、一个不提供静态上下文的消融设置和一个朴素模板基线，共生成3,700套测试工件。工件在真实构建环境中结合运行时检查器编译，并通过“编译器反馈闭环”修复；成功可执行者再进入模糊测试。

Result: 首次编译失败中，80%源于依赖连接、构建配置和接口适配等工程集成问题，而非测试逻辑本身。推理型模型的测试桩首次编译成功率为64%，显著高于代码专用模型的6%。推理型模型虽可经大量桩替代达到对象文件级完全可编译，但不足一半测试桩能够实际进入模糊测试。观察到的37次崩溃均发生在替代桩代码中，而不是Autoware原始代码；在实验预算内，没有任何静态候选弱点得到动态确认。

Conclusion: LLM辅助全栈动态分析的核心障碍是构建集成，而不是候选点发现或后续模糊测试。仅以“能生成测试代码”或“对象文件可编译”衡量能力会高估其实用性；未来系统应重点建模真实构建图、依赖关系、初始化协议和接口契约，并限制替代桩对执行真实性的破坏，才能提高动态验证结果的可信度。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles depend on large safety-critical software stacks, where weaknesses reachable from adversarial inputs may affect steering, braking, or other control decisions. Static analysis can identify candidate sites, but dynamically confirming exploitability requires executable test artifacts that are difficult to construct manually. We investigate whether large language models (LLMs) can automate this process for Autoware, an open-source autonomous-driving stack. We perform compiler-precise static analysis across 185 packages, identifying 1,375 decision rules, 2,274 validation checks, and 482 input-to-safety-output flows, from which we derive a weakness taxonomy and sample 740 reachable sites. Two local open-weight LLMs, a no-static-context ablation, and a naive-template baseline generate 3,700 artifact sets, which are compiled against the real build under sanitizers, repaired through compiler-in-the-loop feedback, and fuzzed when executable. The main result is a build-integration failure taxonomy showing that 80% of first-shot compilation failures arise from dependency wiring rather than program logic. The reasoning model compiled 64% of harnesses on the first attempt, compared with 6% for the code-specialized model. Repair achieved full object-compileability for the reasoning model only through extensive stubbing; fewer than half of its harnesses reached the fuzzer, and all 37 observed crashes originated in stubbed code rather than Autoware. No candidate weakness was dynamically confirmed within budget. These results show that build integration, not candidate generation or fuzzing, is the primary barrier to reliable LLM-assisted dynamic analysis of full autonomous-vehicle software stacks.

</details>

### [14] [A Rig of Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12409)
*Emma Tye*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出以交换半环（commutative rig）刻画有限代数数据类型，并将半环等式解释为数据类型间的同构；同时以部分同构描述类型向更大表示空间的嵌入，如位填充，从而为高层类型建模与底层二进制布局之间建立形式化联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高层函数式语言通常难以让程序员精确控制数据的运行时表示，但实际系统常因性能、存储效率或既有二进制格式兼容而需要区分“程序层的数据结构”与“二进制层的数据布局”。现有布局描述与转换方案需同时满足正确性、效率和易用性，因而需要统一且可验证的理论模型。

Method: 将有限代数数据类型的组合结构映射到交换半环：和类型对应加法，积类型对应乘法，单位类型及空类型对应相应常量；将满足半环恒等式的类型重写解释为显式数据同构，以保证转换可逆。对于不能覆盖目标类型全部值域的布局扩张，则使用部分同构表达源类型与目标类型有效子集之间的双向对应关系。

Result: 该框架能够统一表达代数数据类型的等价变换，以及诸如通过额外比特扩展表示空间的非满射嵌入。其核心贡献是把类型代数定律从抽象等式提升为可执行或可实现的数据转换证据，使布局变换具备明确的正确性语义。摘要未报告具体实现、性能测量或实验比较结果。

Conclusion: 交换半环与数据同构为有限代数数据类型的布局描述和转换提供了简洁的形式化基础；部分同构进一步覆盖了填充等存在无效表示的场景。该方法有望支持兼顾类型安全、表示效率与二进制兼容性的布局控制机制，但其实用性仍取决于具体语言集成、自动化程度及运行时开销。

Abstract: In high-level functional languages, the compiler often gives users little control over the runtime representation of data types. Yet how we model data structures at the program level can be different to how we want to represent them at the binary level, for efficiency or legacy reasons. Hence being able to describe data layouts and their transformations for data types is a useful and necessary part of programming, but difficult to do correctly, efficiently and ergonomically. We present a model of finite algebraic data types as a commutative rig (a ring without additive inverses), where the rig-equalities are modelled by isomorphisms between data. Using this approach, we can also model embedding a data type into a larger type (e.g. bit-padding) as a partial isomorphism.

</details>


### [15] [Stream-based Online and Offline Monitoring under Measurement Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13211)
*Bernd Finkbeiner,Martin Fränzle,Florian Kohn,Paul Kröger*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出RLola，一种面向含测量噪声与误差的鲁棒流式运行时验证语言，并给出在线与离线监控算法，以提高网络物理系统安全断言验证的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统流式监控通常假设传感器读数完全准确，但实际测量噪声会在后续处理和聚合过程中被放大；区间算术又存在别名问题，因此需要一种能够显式建模测量不确定性的验证方法。

Method: RLola通过引入松弛变量符号化表示测量噪声，避免区间算术的别名问题。研究分别设计在线和离线监控算法：在线算法处理实时数据流；离线算法基于满足性模理论分析完整轨迹，判断是否存在一条满足所有断言的真实轨迹。此外，作者识别出RLola的一个丰富片段，可自动转换为具有常数内存保证的在线监控器，并将实现集成到RTLola框架中。

Result: 实验基于一个综合示例评估了算法的精度与运行时间。结果表明，RLola能够处理带测量误差的监控问题；离线算法可发现在线算法可能遗漏的违反情况。对于满足所识别片段条件的规范，在线监控可保证常数内存使用。

Conclusion: RLola为含噪传感器数据的流式运行时验证提供了统一且更鲁棒的解决方案。在线算法适合实时反馈，离线算法适合对完整轨迹进行更全面的可满足性分析；两者结合能够在效率与验证完备性之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Stream-based monitoring is a runtime verification approach for cyber-physical systems that translates streams of input data, such as sensor readings, into streams of aggregate statistics and verdicts about the safety of the system. It is usually assumed that the values on the input streams represent fully accurate measurements of the physical world. In reality, however, physical sensors are prone to measurement noise and errors. These errors are further amplified by the processing and aggregation steps within the monitor. This paper introduces RLola, a robust extension of the stream-based specification language Lola. RLola incorporates the concept of slack variables, which symbolically represent measurement noise while avoiding the aliasing problem of interval arithmetic.
  We present algorithms for both online and offline monitoring of RLola specifications. Since monitoring RLola specifications may require unbounded memory in general, we identify a rich fragment of RLola that can be automatically translated into monitors with guaranteed constant memory usage for online monitoring. An online RLola monitor observes a live system and provides real-time feedback on the current status of specified assertions. A satisfiability-modulo-theories-based offline algorithm analyzes complete system traces and determines whether a hypothetical ground-truth trace exists that satisfies all assertions at all time points. The offline algorithm can therefore detect violations that the online algorithm may miss. We implement these algorithms in the existing RTLola framework and evaluate their precision and running time based on a comprehensive example.

</details>


### [16] [Multiobjective Preexpectation Reasoning for Probabilistic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13268)
*Lena Verscht,Hannah Mertens,Kevin Batz,Sebastian Junges,Benjamin Lucien Kaminski,Joost-Pieter Katoen*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出面向含非确定性的概率程序的多目标策略综合框架：在程序语义层面同时优化多个期望目标，刻画其帕累托前沿，并支持无限状态空间对应的MDP模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统概率程序验证常以单一目标的最弱前期望为核心，难以直接表达多个相互权衡的优化指标。多目标规划需要确定所有可同时达成的目标值，并构造能实现这些值的策略，因此需要兼具语义严谨性与程序级可操作性的推理方法。

Method: 论文定义多目标前期望变换器，将后期望元组映射为可同时实现的值集合；该集合属于凸Hoare幂域。该变换器保守扩展最弱前期望语义，并提升标准循环推理规则。作者进一步给出策略综合规则：通过对非概率确定化程序进行随机混合，构造实现目标值的混合确定化策略。最后，以操作语义下的MDP作为参照，证明变换器与策略综合规则的健全性，且不依赖有限状态假设。

Result: 该框架能够在符号化的程序层面计算或推导多目标可达值，并为相应解提供见证策略。理论上，它将多目标优化、凸值集合语义和概率程序演算统一起来；适用范围覆盖无限状态MDP，而非仅限于有限模型。多个案例研究用于展示规则和工具链的表达能力。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是为多目标概率程序规划建立了一个可验证、可综合策略的演绎式语义框架。其价值在于：既保留最弱前期望与循环规则的兼容性，又通过凸集合和随机混合策略准确处理多目标权衡，为无限状态系统的程序级多目标优化提供了基础。

Abstract: Probabilistic programs with nondeterminism model planning problems in which a strategy resolves the nondeterminism to optimize an expected outcome. We study the multiobjective setting, optimizing several outcomes at once along a Pareto front, and provide a deductive, program-level account of strategy synthesis. Its core is a multiobjective preexpectation transformer mapping a tuple of postexpectations to the set of simultaneously achievable values, an element of the convex Hoare powerdomain. It conservatively extends weakest preexpectations and lifts standard loop rules. We develop rules to synthesize witnessing strategies as mixed determinizations that randomize over non-probabilistic determinizations. We prove the transformer and synthesis rules sound against an operational MDP semantics, without requiring a finite state space: our approach can be seen as a symbolic approach - at program level - for multiobjective optimization over infinite MDPs. We demonstrate our machinery using various case studies.

</details>

### [17] [Synchronous Observers Revisited for Runtime Verification of Lustre Using STL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12693)
*Logan Kenwright,Partha Roop,Sobhan Chatterjee,Nathan Allen*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将同步信号时间逻辑（SSTL）编译为Lustre同步观察器的方法，使同一形式化规范同时支持运行时验证和静态验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同步语言通常使用可组合的同步观察器表达安全属性，但SSTL到此类观察器的编译能力有限，尤其难以处理任意嵌套的有界性质及在线监测所需的全局无界外层算子。

Method: 作者将SSTL的同步片段模块化编译为Lustre数据流语言中的同步观察器，支持任意嵌套的有界SSTL性质，并允许外层使用全局无界算子进行在线监测。生成的观察器可用于运行时验证，也可与Kind2模型检查器结合进行静态验证；此外实现了可交互可视化工具，用于在可编辑轨迹上呈现三值判定结果。

Result: 方法在弹簧—质量系统和汽车跟驰巡航控制器两个文献案例上进行了评估。实验展示了所生成观察器在运行时监测与静态验证中的适用性，并验证了可视化工具对轨迹分析和性质判定的支持能力。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了SSTL向同步观察器的编译能力，统一连接了形式化规范、运行时验证和静态模型检查。模块化编译、嵌套性质支持及全局无界监测算子提高了方法的表达能力和实用性。

Abstract: Signal Temporal Logic (STL) is a popular formalism for the temporal safety properties of cyber-physical systems, most often used for runtime verification. In the synchronous family of languages, safety properties are instead expressed as synchronous observers, modules composed with a program for static verification, which are also runnable specifications suitable for runtime verification, though this use is rarely explored. We present a technique for compiling the synchronous fragment of STL (SSTL) into synchronous observers in the dataflow language Lustre. Unlike previous work, we allow arbitrary nesting of bounded SSTL properties via modular compilation, and admit a globally unbounded outer operator for online monitoring; the resulting observers serve both runtime verification and, as a by-product, static verification with the Kind2 model checker. We further contribute an interactive visualiser that renders a property's three-valued verdict over an editable trace, and evaluate on two case studies from the literature: a spring-mass system and a car-following cruise controller.

</details>


### [18] [Computing Fixed Points using Dependency Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13020)
*Giorgio Bacci,Giovanni Bacci,Kim G. Larsen,Daniele Toller*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出了用于求解带底元的诺特偏序集上方程组的全局与局部算法，通过依赖预言机按需探索与目标变量相关的方程部分，在保证正确性的同时兼顾精度和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多验证任务可归约为偏序结构上的方程组求解。传统方法常需进行较大范围的全局探索，而实际需求通常只关心某个变量的解值，因此需要一种能够局部、按需且可证明终止的通用求解框架。

Method: 方法以依赖预言机为核心：预言机用于推导变量间依赖关系，指导局部固定点计算中的状态探索，并提供可靠的终止判据。该框架同时支持全局和局部求解；预言机可被定制、组合或采用过近似，从而以统一机制实现精度与运行效率之间的可控权衡。

Result: 作者将原型实现与已有文献中的算法进行比较。摘要称，该实现具有竞争力，并且在不少场景下优于专用求解方案，同时保持实现简洁，并能适应多种应用领域。

Conclusion: 依赖预言机提供了一种通用、模块化的局部方程组求解机制。其主要贡献在于：仅探索求解目标所需部分、保证局部固定点计算的可靠终止，并允许通过可替换的预言机灵活调节分析精度和性能而不损害正确性。

Abstract: We present global and local algorithms for solving systems of equations over Noetherian posets with a bottom element, a general setting underlying many verification problems. Our algorithms compute the solution of a selected variable by restricting exploration to those parts of the system required to determine its value. We achieve this by computing variable dependencies by means of dependency oracles. Oracles guide the exploration of the system and provide sound termination criteria for local fixed-point computation. A key advantage of our approach is its flexibility: oracles can be customized, composed, or over-approximated, offering a principled way to trade precision for performance without compromising correctness. We evaluate our solution against existing algorithms from the literature and show that our prototype implementation is competitive and often outperforms specialized solutions, while remaining simple and adaptable across diverse application domains.

</details>


### [19] [Completeness and incompleteness of basic matching logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13306)
*Xiaohong Chen,Grigore Rosu*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究不带定义性和不动点的基本匹配逻辑的全局完备性，并刻画多排序、最小不动点及混合逻辑中名词符号对完备性的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 明确匹配逻辑在任意一排序有限元签名下是否满足全局完备性，并确定定义性扩展、不动点、多排序和名词符号对可公理化性与完备性的影响。

Method: 将任意理论Γ局部化为理论Δ_Γ，把语义蕴涵和形式推导都约化为同一局部关系；通过双覆盖构造证明语义等价性。进一步利用不可递归枚举性、排序流分析以及对满足局部化约束的良基演算的障碍分析，建立负面结果。

Result: 无不动点的一排序基本匹配逻辑满足全局完备性：Γ⊨φ当且仅当Γ⊢φ，并推出定义性扩展的保守性。加入最小不动点后，有效公理化性失效；在含一个一元符号和两个二元符号且无常量的签名中，有效式不可递归枚举。三排序下，即使Γ可满足，全局完备性也可能失败。对混合逻辑而言，无名词符号时，带存在与全称状态变量量词、任意元模态的语言具有全局完备性；加入名词符号后，指定的良基演算类均无法全局完备。

Conclusion: 全局完备性在一排序、无不动点的基本匹配逻辑中成立，但这一结论在排序数量、固定点构造和名词符号扩展下具有明确边界。其失效主要源于排序流、固定点带来的有效性复杂度，以及名词符号与局部化之间的结构性冲突。

Abstract: Basic matching logic is matching logic without definedness. Symbols are interpreted as set-valued operations, element variables denote singletons and are bound by $\exists$, and no connective uniformly internalizes totality. For basic matching logic without fixpoints over an arbitrary one-sorted finitary signature, we prove \emph{global completeness} ($Γ\vDash\varphi$ iff $Γ\vdash\varphi$, for arbitrary, possibly infinite $Γ$) and, as a corollary, conservativity of the definedness extension. The proof localizes $Γ$ to a theory $Δ_Γ$ and reduces semantic consequence and derivability to the same local relation: $Γ\vDash\varphi$ iff $Δ_Γ\vDash_\text{loc}\varphi$ iff $Γ\vdash\varphi$. A double-cover construction establishes the semantic equivalence. Least fixpoints destroy effective axiomatizability. Over a signature with one unary and two binary symbols and no constants, validity is not recursively enumerable; hence no sound calculus with a recursively enumerable proof relation is even weakly complete, already for the empty theory and without definedness. The positive result is also sharp in the number of sorts. Global completeness fails with three sorts for a satisfiable $Γ$. Thus the completeness conjecture holds for one sort and fails in general. The negative results arise from sort flow, fixpoint effectivity, and, for hybrid logic, an obstruction to every well-founded calculus whose leaves are hypotheses or valid patterns and whose rules respect localization. This yields a matching-logic-independent dichotomy: the language with state variables bound by $\exists$ and $\forall$ over modalities of arbitrary arity is globally complete without nominals, while no calculus in that well-founded class is globally complete once nominals are added.

</details>


### [20] [A Dense Weisfeiler-Leman Algorithm for Deciding Bounded-Cliquewidth Homomorphism Indistinguishability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13382)
*Radu Curticapean,Daniel Neuen,Amir Nikabadi,Tim Seppelt,Ben Young*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文研究“相对于图类的同态不可区分性”的可判定性，首次给出针对稠密图类的一般性算法结果：对团宽不超过k的图类，该关系可由新提出的稠密Weisfeiler-Leman算法判定；对有界团宽的CMSO_1可定义图类亦可判定，并给出相应复杂度界与PSPACE紧性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同态计数统一刻画了多种图同构松弛，如经典同构、k维Weisfeiler-Leman等价及量子同构。由于测试图类通常无穷，是否能判定两个图在该类上的同态计数完全一致并不显然；已有可判定结果主要局限于稀疏图类，而平面图类上甚至不可判定。因此，解决稠密图类上的可判定性是关键缺口。

Method: 作者针对团宽这一稠密图参数提出稠密Weisfeiler-Leman算法。与标准WL细化顶点k元组不同，该算法细化顶点子集的k元组颜色，以捕捉由有界团宽图作为源图时的同态计数信息。进一步，论文建立元定理，将判定范围扩展至任意CMSO_1可定义且团宽有界的图类；对于线性团宽有界的情形，结合更受限的构造结构获得更低的空间复杂度上界。

Result: 稠密WL算法能够判定两个图是否在所有团宽至多k的图上同态不可区分。更一般地，任意CMSO_1可定义的有界团宽图类上的同态不可区分性可在随机化指数时间内判定。若图类具有有界线性团宽，则问题属于PSPACE；作者还构造了一个此类图类，使该判定问题达到PSPACE完全，从而证明该复杂度界紧确。

Conclusion: 论文填补了同态不可区分性在稠密图类上的算法空白，表明有界团宽及其逻辑可定义子类支持有效判定。其核心贡献是以“顶点子集元组”替代“顶点元组”的稠密WL框架，并通过元定理和PSPACE完全性刻画了该方向的可判定边界与复杂度。

Abstract: Two graphs $G$ and $H$ are homomorphism indistinguishable over a graph class $\mathcal{F}$ if they admit the same number of homomorphisms from every graph in $\mathcal{F}$. A wide range of relaxations of graph isomorphism arise this way: isomorphism itself over the class of all graphs [Lovász, Acta Math. Hung. 1967], equivalence under the $k$-dimensional Weisfeiler-Leman algorithm over the graphs of treewidth $\leq k$ [Dvořák, J. Graph Theory 2010], and quantum isomorphism over planar graphs [Mančinska-Roberson, FOCS 2020]. Since the class $\mathcal{F}$ is typically infinite, it is not clear a priori whether homomorphism indistinguishability over $\mathcal{F}$ is decidable; for planar graphs it is undecidable. Every class for which decidability was previously known is sparse.
  We give the first decidability results for dense graph classes: We introduce the dense Weisfeiler-Leman algorithm that decides homomorphism indistinguishability over the class of graphs of cliquewidth $\leq k$, the dense counterpart of treewidth. This relation was not previously known to be decidable. The algorithm colors $k$-tuples of vertex subsets rather than $k$-tuples of vertices.
  Beyond the class of all graphs of cliquewidth $\leq k$, we prove a general meta-theorem: homomorphism indistinguishability over every $\mathsf{CMSO}_1$-definable graph class of bounded cliquewidth is decidable, in randomized exponential time. For classes of bounded linear cliquewidth the bound improves to $\mathsf{PSPACE}$, and we show this is tight by exhibiting such a class for which the problem is $\mathsf{PSPACE}$-complete. These are the first general algorithms for homomorphism indistinguishability over dense graph classes.

</details>


### [21] [Runtime Monitoring of Distributed Cyber-Physical Systems Without a Global Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13486)
*Charles Koll,Houssam Abbas*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文针对各节点本地时钟存在漂移、缺乏确定全局时间的分布式信息物理系统，首次给出了对稠密时间时序逻辑规范进行连续监测的理论刻画与算法。算法能够计算所有可能使规范成立的全局时刻集合，并可扩展至多智能体场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式信息物理系统通常由多个具有独立本地时钟的节点构成，时钟漂移使得传统基于统一时间轴的时序语义难以直接适用。现有分布式监测方法多面向离散时间，或要求预先将时间约束显式映射到各本地时钟，因此难以满足连续动态系统的监测需求。

Method: 作者面向一个仍保留全部时间算子的信号时序逻辑片段，定义了适用于部分同步时钟环境的扩展满足信号语义；进一步分析多维部分同步时间空间的几何结构，并据此设计监测算法。该算法以各节点观测与时钟不确定性为输入，推导规范可能成立的全局时间点集合；同时给出最坏情况复杂度，并实现其保守且可靠的近似版本。

Result: 理论上，工作提供了分布式连续时间监测的首个形式化刻画和算法，并确定了算法的最坏情况复杂度。实验上，所实现的可靠近似方法展示出有效的监测能力，在最多50个节点的场景中仍可运行。

Conclusion: 该研究将稠密时间时序逻辑监测扩展到时钟漂移的分布式信息物理系统，使设计者能够在不存在确定全局时间的条件下识别规范可能满足的全局时刻。该能力可用于分布式混合控制系统的调试、诊断与行为验证。

Abstract: We give the first theoretical characterization, and the first algorithm, for continuous monitoring of a distributed Cyber-Physical System (CPS) against a dense-time temporal logic specification. A distributed CPS is composed of multiple agents, each with a local clock; these clocks drift from each other, so there is no well-defined global time. When monitoring such a system's output signal against a temporal logic specification, it is not evident how to interpret the temporal constraints of the formula, and what satisfaction means. Yet CPS designers, like control engineers, typically think of their system's operation in terms of global time. Most existing techniques for monitoring distributed systems work with discrete-time specifications not suitable for CPS, and/or require an explicit mapping of temporal constraints to local clocks. We introduce an algorithm that addresses the above challenges for a fragment of Signal Temporal Logic (STL) that still includes all temporal operators. It relies on a novel extension of satisfaction signals to this partially synchronous setting (where clocks drift), and an analysis of the geometry of multi-dimensional partially synchronous time. The algorithm returns the set of all possible global moments that can satisfy the specification. Knowledge of these possible global moments is important for debugging distributed hybrid control systems such as fleets of drones and electrical grids. We derive the worst-case complexity of the algorithm, and implement a sound approximation of it that experimentally illustrates effective monitoring, even in scenarios of up to 50 agents.

</details>
